# Roth/GenWronskian.lean / Roth/HasseDeriv.lean

475 comparisons among the 293 accepted declarations. [Source hashes and review provenance](GenWronskian--HasseDeriv.json).

| `MvPolynomial.hasseCoeff` | `Roth.genWronskian` | no | A: Defines the coordinatewise binomial coefficient for two finitely supported natural multi-indices, multiplying only over the differentiation index support. Mechanism: Uses Finsupp.prod with Nat.choose; omitted coordinates contribute choose(k,0)=1, so the finite-support presentation gives the full multivariate binomial factor. B: Defines the determinant whose rows apply prescribed multivariate Hasse derivatives to a finite polynomial family. Mechanism: Forms a square Matrix.of of hasseDeriv entries and takes its determinant. The operator API gives individual divided derivatives and composition. Generalized Wronskians select finite families of such operators, while Kronecker substitution preserves bounded-degree information and its derivative span uses polynomial scalar coefficients. Closure under differentiating a span needs the derivative product rule on those scalar coefficients; generator derivative formulas alone are insufficient. A nonvanishing determinant is not implied by one derivative identity. | Use the shared derivative formulas as explicit inputs; preserve finite-support substitution, span and row-selection results independently. |

A nonvanishing determinant is not implied by one derivative identity. | Use the shared derivative formulas as explicit inputs; preserve finite-support substitution, span and row-selection results independently. |
